I've just noticed my clothes and wedding ring are quite loose, which honestly gives me a quiet sense of satisfaction.\n\nDoctor: Even if you haven't tracked it closely, do you know when you had your last menstrual period, or if you have missed your periods for three or more consecutive months?\n\nPatient: I'm really not sure of the exact timing because I haven't been tracking it closely at all. I preferred to look away when they had me step on the scale, so I didn't actually see what it was.\n\nDoctor: To help me understand the extent of the change, do you know approximately how many pounds you have lost since you began restricting your eating?"

What brings you in to see me?\n\nPatient: I’ve gotten so strict about what and when I eat that it feels like I’m constantly cutting back and still telling myself it isn’t enough. I also constantly obsess over looking lean and flat, especially during professional presentations where I feel like my credibility as a scientist is tied to looking disciplined and thin.\n\nDoctor: To help me better understand your physical health, could you share your current height and weight, and whether you have lost a significant amount of weight recently?\n\nPatient: I don't really track the exact numbers, but I have definitely lost weight recently; my lab coat is hanging off me and my wedding ring spins on my finger. I'm just tired from grant deadlines, and honestly, managing to shrink a bit gives me a sense of satisfaction rather than concern.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed physical changes like feeling constantly cold, or if you menstruate, have your periods stopped or become irregular?\n\nPatient: I haven't really noticed or paid attention to things like feeling cold or changes to my cycle, to be honest. I've just noticed my clothes and wedding ring are quite loose, which honestly gives me a quiet sense of satisfaction.\n\nDoctor: Even if you haven't tracked it closely, do you know when you had your last menstrual period, or if you have missed your periods for three or more consecutive months?\n\nPatient: I'm really not sure of the exact timing because I haven't been tracking it closely at all.
